My mind can feel busy and restless, but it's not really filled with those kinds of anxious thoughts.\n\nDoctor: Over the last year and a half, have you been feeling down, depressed, or lost interest or pleasure in things you normally enjoy?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't really been feeling depressed or down at all. I also haven't lost interest in my hobbies or the things I usually enjoy.\n\nDoctor: Do you find it difficult to stay seated when you are expected to, or do you feel like you are constantly 'on the go' as if driven by a motor?\n\nPatient: Yes, absolutely. I find it really difficult to stay seated for long periods of time, and I constantly feel this inner urge to keep moving. It's like I always have this restless energy inside me that makes it hard to just sit still and relax.\n\nDoctor: Do you often find yourself easily distracted by things going on around you, or do you struggle with organizing your tasks and keeping track of deadlines?\n\nPatient: I'm not quite sure about those, to be honest. I haven't really noticed having issues with external distractions or keeping my tasks organized.\n\nDoctor: Do you often find yourself fidgeting with your hands or feet, tapping them, or squirming in your seat when you are trying to sit still?"
  }

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
I haven't really noticed myself fidgeting or tapping my feet all that much. I'm not really sure if I do that when trying to sit still, to be honest.
